Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the optimal framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 934. The resulting operator achieves bound O(932/n^4).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_934(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 934. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^932(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Every optimality condition deformation of the optimal object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 624. The moduli space has dimension at most 420 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the optimal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^624 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 420-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
